                                                             COMPANY PROFILE

本集團是中國包裝飲用水行業和中國即飲軟         The Group is a renowned company in China’s packaged drinking
飲行業的知名企業。歷經40餘年的發展，本        water and ready-to-drink soft beverage industry. After over
集團已成長為中國第二大包裝飲用水企業以         four decades of development, the Group has grown into the
及中國最大的飲用純淨水企業。以本集團的         second largest company in the packaged drinking water market
「怡寶」品牌為中樞，以飲用純淨水產品的成        in China and the largest company in the purified drinking water
功為根基，本集團通過豐富水種、拓展產品         market in China. Anchored on its “C’estbon” (怡寶) brand, and
規格，不斷豐富本集團的包裝飲用水產品矩         rooted in the success of its purified drinking water products, the
陣，覆蓋包括外出、居家、商務、餐飲、運         Group persistently enhances its product portfolio of packaged
動等多種消費場景。                   drinking water products through diversification of water types
                            and expansion of product specifications, catering to a wide range
                            of consumption scenarios including outdoor, indoor, business,
                            catering and sports.


2024 年10 月23 日，本集團於聯交所主板成   On October 23, 2024, the Group was successfully listed on the
功上市，2024 年亦是本集團成立40 周年，     Main Board of the Stock Exchange. The year 2024 also marks the
此次在聯交所掛牌上市，是本集團面向新時         40th anniversary of the Group’s establishment, and the Group’s
代、推動新發展的戰略選擇，也是發展歷程         success in listing on the Stock Exchange represents its strategic
中的又一個重要里程碑。                 initiative to embrace a new era and promote new development,
                            serving as a significant milestone in its evolution.

                                                                              二零二四年年報

                                                                         ANNUAL REPORT 2024
     管理層討論與分析
     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS

     宏觀及行業環境                                         MACRO AND INDUSTRY ENVIRONMENT
     2024年全球經濟環境仍舊複雜多變，為了應                           In 2024, the global economic landscape continued to be
     對國際形勢的諸多不確定性，國內推出一系                             complex and volatile. In light of the numerous uncertainties
     列消費政策，在政策刺激下國內消費市場總                             in the international environment, a range of consumption
     體平穩，全年社會零售總額48.8萬億元人民                           policies have been introduced domestically. As a result of these
     幣，較去年同比增長3.5%，但各細分行業表                           policy interventions, the domestic consumer market remained
     現存在分化，飲料商品零售呈震蕩態勢。                              generally stable with the total social retail sales value for the
                                                     year amounting to RMB48.8 trillion, representing a year-on-year
                                                     increase of 3.5% compared to the previous year. However, the
                                                     performance across various sub-sectors has varied, the retail
                                                     sale of beverage showed a vibration trend.


     業務回顧                                            BUSINESS REVIEW
     本集團致力於成為滿足中國消費者基本及多                             Committed to becoming the go-to brand to address the basic
     樣化飲用需求的首選品牌，不斷在多元化                              and diversified beverage needs of Chinese consumers, the Group
     的品牌組合下開發及提供各種高品質即飲                              has been developing and offering a variety of quality ready-

位，打破南強北弱局面，提升長江沿岸省份        market and exploring the incremental market” (保存量，攻
市場份額，推動西北市場高速增長。2024       變量，尋增量) to further consolidate its leading position in
年在包裝飲用水業務上，持續提升根據地市        advantageous markets, improve the imbalance in market
場業務執行能力，發揮市場品牌效應，進一        strength between northern and southern China, increase market
步擴大領先優勢；深度開發長江沿岸下沉         share in provinces along the Yangtze River and promote rapid

代渠道業務，實現業務穩健增長，區域重要        business in 2024, we have continued to improve our business

品牌建設                       BRAND BUILDING
面對地域廣闊、品牌眾多、資源有限的實際        Faced with the practical challenges of vast territory, numerous
挑戰，本集團採取「聚焦資源、集中發力」        brands and limited resources, the Group has adopted the
的戰略方針，秉持長期主義的理念。本集         strategic approach of “focusing on resources and concentrating
團將大型戶外廣告作為主要宣傳方式，連         on efforts” (聚焦資源、集中發力) and adhered to the concept

外匯風險                                     Foreign Exchange Risk

本集團的大部分收入和大部分支出均以人民                      The majority of the Group’s revenue and expenditures are
幣計價。本集團的附屬公司在中國大陸及香                      denominated in Renminbi. The Group’s subsidiaries operate in
港營運，並分別以當地貨幣作為其功能貨幣                      Mainland China and Hong Kong, with their functional currencies
（即人民幣及港元）。本集團進行若干以外幣                     being the local currencies (i.e., RMB and HKD), respectively. The
計值的交易，因此產生匯率波動風險。本集                      Group engages in certain transactions denominated in foreign
團目前並無外幣對沖政策。然而，管理層會                      currencies, thereby exposing it to exchange rate fluctuation risks.
監察外匯風險，並將於有需要時考慮對沖重                      Currently, the Group does not have a foreign currency hedging
大外幣風險。                                   policy in place. However, the management actively monitors
                                         foreign exchange risks and will consider hedging significant
                                         foreign currency exposures when necessary.

                            CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT

董事會欣然提呈本公司截至2024 年12 月31    The Board is pleased to submit the Corporate Governance Report
日止年度之企業管治報告。                of the Company for the year ended December 31, 2024.


企業文化                        CORPORATE CULTURE
本集團以「十四五」華潤企業文化理念體系         Based on the “14th Five-Year Plan” corporate culture philosophy
為基石，與業務目標緊密相連，推動企業穩         of China Resources, the Group closely adheres to its business
健前行。本集團堅持引領行業發展，以「健         objectives to drive its stable advancement. The Group insists
康、安全、專業」為品牌形象，為廣大消費         on leading industry development and providing the quality
者提供優質產品，不斷深入挖掘市場增長潛         products to consumers with “healthy, safe and professionalism”
力，致力於為消費者提供更加多元化的選擇         as the brand image, and continuously explores market growth
與更高品質的產品。本集團以「誠實守信、         potential, striving to offer consumers more diversified choices
業績導向、以人為本、合作共贏」為企業價值        and higher quality products. With the corporate values of
觀，打造真誠、團結、開放、進取的組織氛         “integrity, performance-orientation, people-oriented, and win-
圍，將企業文化融入到企業日常運營中，成         win cooperation”, the Group fosters an organizational atmosphere
為指導員工行為的內在動力。               of sincerity, solidarity, open-mindedness and proactivity, and
                            integrates corporate culture into its daily operations to make it an
                            intrinsic motivation that guides employees’ behaviors.


企業管治常規                      CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES
本公司深知在本集團管理架構及內部控制程         The Company is well aware of the importance of incorporating
序引入優良的企業管治元素的重要性，藉以         excellent corporate governance elements into the Group’s
達致有效的問責性。                   management structure and internal control process to achieve
                            effective accountability.


本公司已應用企業管治守則所載的原則並採         The Company has applied the principles outlined in the Corporate
納當中所述的守則條文。本公司堅信，董事         Governance Code and adopted the code provisions described
會中執行董事與獨立非執行董事的組合應保         therein. The Company firmly believes that the composition of
持平衡，以使董事會有強大的獨立性，能夠         executive Directors and independent non-executive Directors
有效作出獨立判斷。                   of the Board should be balanced to ensure the Board maintains
                            strong independence and can effectively make independent
                            judgments.


本公司於上市日期起直至2024 年12 月31 日   The Company has complied with all applicable code provisions
期間一直遵守企業管治守則列載之所有適用         set forth in the Corporate Governance Code from the Listing Date
守則條文。                       until December 31, 2024.

     董事會及管理層之職責、問責及貢獻                                DUTIES, ACCOUNTABILITY AND CONTRIBUTIONS OF
                                                     THE BOARD AND MANAGEMENT
     董事會為本公司主要的決策組織，負責監督                             The Board is the main decision-making body of the Company,
     本集團業務、策略決策及表現，並共同負責                             responsible for overseeing the Group’s business, strategic
     透過指引及監督本公司事務以促使其成功。                             decisions and performance, and collectively accountable for
     董事會作出客觀決策以保障本公司及其股東                             facilitating the Company’s success through guidance and
     的利益。董事會授予本集團高級管理層有關                             supervision of its affairs. The Board makes objective decisions to
     本集團日常管理及營運的權限及職責。                               safeguard the interests of the Company and its Shareholders. The
                                                     Board grants the senior management of the Group the authority
                                                     and duties related to the daily management and operations of the
                                                     Group.


     全體董事（包括獨立非執行董事）均為董事                             All the Directors (including independent non-executive Directors)
     會提供多種領域的寶貴業務經驗、知識及專                             shall provide the Board with valuable business experience,
     長，使其高效及有效地運作。                                   knowledge and expertise in various fields, enabling it to operate
                                                     efficiently and effectively.

     息政策或預設的股息支付率。股息的分派由                             of dividends that may be declared or paid by our Group in the

     或支付任何股息的決定及其金額取決於多項                             pre-determined dividend payout ratio. Distribution of dividends
     因素，包括但不限於本集團的經營業績、現                             shall be decided by our Board at their discretion in compliance
     金流量及財務狀況、經營及資本開支需求、                             with the applicable laws and regulations. A decision to declare

立非執行董事有權就董事會會議上將予討論         Directors who are entitled to request additional information and

保密方式進行溝通。彼等亦可向本公司之公         non-executive Directors to share their perspectives and
司秘書尋求協助。若彼等或其任何緊密聯繫         communicate in a confidential way when necessary. They may
人於任何合約或安排中擁有重大利益，則該         also seek assistance from the company secretary of the Company.
獨立非執行董事不得就通過該合約或安排之         In any instance where they or any of their close associates have a
董事決議案進行投票，亦不得計入該會議之         material interest in a contract or arrangement, the independent
法定人數。上述機制旨在確保董事會擁有堅         non-executive Directors are prohibited from voting on the
固之獨立元素，並能夠取得獨立意見及觀          Board resolution related to that contract or arrangement and
點。截至2024 年12 月31 日止年度，所有獨   will not be counted towards the quorum for that meeting. This
立非執行董事均悉數出席了相關董事委員會         mechanism aims to ensure that the Board maintains a strong
會議及董事會會議，並積極參與董事會之決         level of independence and can secure independent opinions
策程序，且在董事會履行其職責時提供了寶         and insights. During the year ended December 31, 2024, all
貴的獨立意見及觀點。基於上述情況，董事         independent non-executive Directors attended all relevant Board
會經全面檢討後，確信其有關取得獨立意見         committee and Board meetings, actively engaged in the Board’s
及觀點之機制已獲全面落實，並持續行之有         decision-making process and provided valuable independent
效。此等機制對本公司之決策質素及企業管         opinions and insights in the performance of duties by the Board.
治水平均構成了不可或缺的保障。             Based on the above, the Board, upon a thorough review, is

     董事對財務報表的責任                                      DIRECTORS’ RESPONSIBILITY FOR THE FINANCIAL
                                                     STATEMENTS
     董事承擔編製本公司截至2024 年12 月31 日                       The Directors acknowledge their responsibility for preparing

     2.     風險管理及內部監控系統的主要特點                         2.    Major Characteristics of Risk Management and Internal
                                                           Monitoring System

            本集團的風險管治及內部監控架構基                               The Group’s structure of risks governance and internal
            於「三道防線」模型，明確風險管理                               monitoring is based on the model of “three lines of
            分工與管理職責。第一道防線，由各                               defense”, which specifies risk management division and
            級業務單位構成，位於業務流程及相                               management responsibility. The first line of defense
            伴生的風險的前端，側重對各項業務                               is constituted with business units at all levels, in the
            相關風險進行直接的、事前和事中的                               front end of business process and associated risks, and
            防範和控制；第二道防線，由風險管                               places emphasis on the direct, prior – and during-event
            理、財務、法務及其他職能共同構                                prevention and control over risks related to each business.
            成，輸出風險相關的理念、體系和方                               The second line of defense is jointly constituted by the
            法，對「第一道防線」的運作進行合理                              risk management, finance, legal and other functional
            的監控和指導，確保「第一道防線」管                              departments, generates risk-related ideas, systems
            理效果的最優化；第三道防線，由本                               and methods, and conducts reasonable monitoring and
            集團董事會下設的審核委員會及內部                               guiding in respect of the function of the “first line of
            審計職能構成，對前兩道防線的運作                               defense”, thus ensuring maximum management results
            進行事後檢查、評估，明確並組織整                               of the “first line of defense”. The third line of defense is
            改存在的問題，確保風險管理各項政                               constituted with the Audit Committee under the Board and
            策、職責的落實及持續優化。通過多                               the internal audit functional departments of the Group,
                                                           performs post-event inspection and assessment to the
                                                           operation of the first two lines, defines and organizes
                                                           rectification for existing problems, so as to guarantee the
                                                           implementation and continuous optimization of various

披露內幕消息                    DISCLOSURE OF INSIDE INFORMATION
本集團重視適當處理和傳閱內幕消息。本集       The Group attaches great importance to the appropriate handling
團已參考證券及期貨事務監察委員會頒佈的       and circulation of inside information. The Group has set up a
內幕消息披露指引，設置有關披露內幕消息       management framework for the disclosure of inside information
的管理框架。在此管理框架下，本集團制定       by reference to the Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside Information
了合理的涉及內幕消息保密、存檔等內容的       issued by the Securities and Futures Commission. Under this
管理制度，設有合理及時地處理和披露內幕       management framework, the Group has established a reasonable
消息的程序。例如下列步驟：管理制度中對       management system that addresses the confidentiality and
內幕消息情形及內幕消息知情人範圍進行了       archiving of inside information, including procedures for
合理規定，內幕消息知情人在涉及內幕消息       the reasonable and timely handling and disclosure of inside
的重大事項的策劃、研究、決策過程中需要       information. For instance, the steps involved are as follows: the
保密，尋求專業意見或諮詢時要求專業機構       management system reasonably defines the circumstances under
履行保密義務，加強對內幕消息知情人檔案       which inside information arises and the scope of individuals
登記管理，向公眾全面披露資料前，內幕消       who have access to inside information; insiders are required
息知情人不得買賣本公司任何證券，內幕消       to maintain confidentiality during the planning, research and
息披露將根據上市規則要求適時履行內部審       decision-making processes concerning significant matters
批流程後進行公開披露。               related to inside information; when seeking professional advice
                          or consultation, it is mandatory for professional institutions
                          to adhere to confidentiality obligations; efforts are made to
                          enhance the registration and management system for files of
                          individuals who have access to inside information; prior to the
                          comprehensive public disclosure of any information, insiders
                          are prohibited from trading the Company’s securities; any inside
                          information will be publicly disclosed in accordance with the
                          Listing Rules after the necessary internal approval processes are
                          completed.

退休福利計劃                    RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES
本集團根據強制性公積金計劃（「強制性公積      The Group operates a defined contribution retirement benefits
金計劃」）為合資格參與強制性公積金計劃的      scheme under the Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the “MPF
僱員設立一項界定供款退休福利計劃。強制       Scheme”) for employees who are eligible to participate in the MPF
性公積金計劃的資產與本集團的資產分開，       Scheme. The assets of the MPF Scheme are held separately from
並由受託人控制的基金持有。本集團按相關       those of the Group, in fund under the control of trustees. The
工資成本的若干百分比向強制性公積金計劃       Group contributes a certain percentage of relevant payroll costs
作出供款，而該等供款由僱員作出。          to the MPF Scheme, which contribution is matched by employees.


本公司於中國成立的附屬公司的僱員為中國       The employees of the Company’s subsidiaries established in the
政府運作的國家管理退休福利計劃的成員。       PRC are members of a state-managed retirement benefit scheme
附屬公司須按工資成本的若干百分比向退休       operated by the PRC government. The subsidiaries are required to
福利計劃供款，以為福利提供資金。本集團       contribute a certain percentage of payroll costs to the retirement
就退休福利計劃的唯一責任為作出特定供款。      benefits schemes to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the
                          Group with respect to the retirement benefit schemes is to make
                          specific contributions.

有關係和其他事項，以及在適用情況下，為     relationships and other matters that may reasonably be thought
消除威脅而採取的行動或應用的防範措施。     to bear on our independence, and where applicable, actions taken
                        to eliminate threats or safeguards applied.


從與治理層溝通的事項中，我們確定對本期     From the matters communicated with those charged with
間合併財務報表的審計最為重要的有關事      governance, we determine those matters that were of most
項，因而構成關鍵審計事項。我們在核數師     significance in the audit of the consolidated financial statements
報告中描述該等事項，除非法律法規不允許     of the current period and are therefore the key audit matters.
公開披露該等事項，或在極端罕見的情況      We describe these matters in our auditor’s report unless law or
下，如果合理預期在我們報告中溝通某事項     regulation precludes public disclosure about the matter or when,
造成的負面後果超過產生的公眾利益，我們     in extremely rare circumstances, we determine that a matter
決定不應在報告中溝通該事項。          should not be communicated in our report because the adverse

     NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS

1.   一般資料                             1.     GENERAL INFORMATION
     華潤飲料（控股）有限公司（「本公司」                      China Resources Beverage (Holdings) Company Limited
     為一家於1995 年7 月4 日註冊成立的                   (the “Company”) is a British Virgin Islands (“BVI”) business
     英屬維爾京群島（「英屬維爾京群島」）                      company with limited liability incorporated on 4 July
     商業有限公司，並於2024年4月16日                     1995 and was re-domiciled from the BVI to the Cayman
     由英屬維爾京群島遷至開曼群島。本                        Islands on 16 April 2024. The shares of the Company
     公司股份自2024 年10 月23 日起於香                  have been listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
     港聯合交易所有限公司上市。其直接                        Limited with effect from 23 October 2024. Its immediate
     控股公司為華潤集團（飲料）有限公司                       holding company is CRH (Beverage) Limited (incorporated
     （於英屬維爾京群島註冊成立），本公                       in the BVI) and the directors of the Company consider the
     司董事認為最終控股公司為於中華人                        ultimate holding company to be China Resources Company

           財務報表。當本公司符合以下                    entities controlled by the Company and its

           制：                               Company:


           •     擁有對被投資者的權                  •      has power over the investee;
                 力；

                 而承擔或有權獲得可變                        from its involvement with the investee; and
                 回報；及

                 的權力影響所得到回報                        returns.
                 的金額。

           三要素中的一項或多項要素發                    an investee if facts and circumstances indicate
           生了改變，本公司將重新評                     that there are changes to one or more of the three

           制。

           業務合併或資產收購                       Business combinations or asset acquisitions

           選擇性集中度測試                        Optional concentration test

           本集團可選擇按逐筆交易基準                   The Group can elect to apply an optional
           採用選擇性集中度測試，以簡                   concentration test, on a transaction-by-transaction
           化對所收購的活動及資產的組                   basis, that permits a simplified assessment of
           合是否屬於業務的評估。倘所                   whether an acquired set of activities and assets
           收購總資產的公允價值幾乎集                   is not a business. The concentration test is met if
           中在某一單獨可識別資產或一                   substantially all of the fair value of the gross assets
           組類似可識別資產上，則該組                   acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable
           合通過集中度測試。評估中的                   asset or group of similar identifiable assets. The
           總資產不包括現金及現金等價                   gross assets under assessment exclude cash and
           物、遞延所得稅資產以及由遞                   cash equivalents, deferred tax assets, and goodwill
           延稅項負債影響形成的商譽。                   resulting from the effects of deferred tax liabilities.
           倘該組合通過集中度測試，則                   If the concentration test is met, the set of activities
           有關活動及資產應被確定為不                   and assets is determined not to be a business and
           構成業務，無需進一步評估。                   no further assessment is needed.


           資產收購                            Asset acquisitions

           當本集團收購並不構成一項業                   When the Group acquires a group of assets and
           務的一組資產及負債時，本集                   liabilities that do not constitute a business, the
           團識別及確認所收購個別可識                   Group identifies and recognises the individual
           別資產及所承擔負債，首先按                   identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed
           其於購買日的相對公允價值將                   by allocating the purchase price first to identifiable
           購買值分配至可識別資產及負                   assets and liabilities on the basis of their relative
           債。有關交易並不會導致出現                   fair values at the date of purchase. Such a
           商譽或議價收購收益。                      transaction does not give rise to goodwill or bargain
                                           purchase gain.

                    性流程，二者共同對創造產出                                 process that together significantly contribute to the

                    程若對持續產生產出的能力至                                 are considered substantive if they are critical to
                    關重要（包括具備執行相關流                                 the ability to continue producing outputs, including
                    程所需技能、知識或經驗的有                                 an organised workforce with the necessary skills,
                    組織團隊）或對持續產生產出                                 knowledge, or experience to perform the related

                    稀有或不花費大量成本、努力                                 ability to continue producing outputs and are
                    或延誤持續生產產出的能力便                                 considered unique or scarce or cannot be replaced
                    無法被取代，則被視為實質性                                 without significant cost, effort, or delay in the ability
                    流程。                                           to continue producing outputs.


                    對業務的收購採用收購法核                                  Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for

                    公允價值計量，公允價值指本                                 transferred in a business combination is measured

                    收購方原擁有人發生的負債以                                 the acquisition-date fair values of the assets
                    及本集團為換取對被收購方控                                 transferred by the Group, liabilities incurred by the
                    制權而發行的股權在收購日期                                 Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the
                    的公允價值之和。收購相關成                                 equity interests issued by the Group in exchange
                    本通常在發生時於損益內確                                  for control of the acquiree. Acquisition-related
                    認。                                            costs are generally recognised in profit or loss as

                    行政用途的有形資產（下文所                                 goods or services, or for administrative purposes

                    房及設備乃按成本減其後累計                                 below). Property, plant and equipment are stated in

                    有）於合併財務狀況表入賬。                                 cost less subsequent accumulated depreciation and
                                                                  subsequent accumulated impairment losses, if any.


                    供生產、供應或行政用途的在                                 Buildings and machinery in the course
                    建樓宇和機器按成本減去任何                                 of construction for production, supply or
                    已確認的減值虧損列賬。成                                  administrative purposes are carried at cost, less
                    本包括任何直接歸屬使有關資                                 any recognised impairment loss. Costs include any
                    產達到必要地點及狀態可按管                                 costs directly attributable to bringing the asset to
                    理層擬定方式營運的成本，及                                 the location and condition necessary for it to be
                   （就合資格資產而言）根據本集                                 capable of operating in the manner intended by

                    他物業資產相同，乃於資產可                                 accounting policy. Depreciation of these assets,

                    物業、廠房及設備、使用權資                                 Impairment on property, plant and equipment,

                    於報告期末，本集團檢討具有                                 At the end of the reporting period, the Group
                    有限使用年期的物業、廠房及                                 reviews the carrying amounts of property, plant

                    釐定減值虧損程度（倘有）。                                 of the relevant asset is estimated in order to
                                                                  determine the extent of the impairment loss (if any).


                    可收回金額為公允價值減出售                                 Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less
                    成本與使用價值之較高者。於                                 costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing
                    評估使用價值時，估計未來現                                 value in use, the estimated future cash flows
                    金流量乃使用稅前貼現率貼現                                 are discounted to their present value using a
                    至其現值，該貼現率反映目前                                 pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market
                    市場對資金時間值之評估以及                                 assessments of the time value of money and the
                    估計未來現金流量未調整之資                                 risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating
                    產（或現金產生單位）的獨有風                                unit (“CGU”)) for which the estimates of future cash
                    險。                                            flows have not been adjusted.


                    倘資產（或現金產生單位）之可                                If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a CGU)
                    收回金額估計少於其賬面值，                                 is estimated to be less than its carrying amount,
                    則 該 資 產（ 或 現 金 產 生 單 位 ）                      the carrying amount of the asset (or a CGU) is
                    之賬面值將撇減至其可收回金                                 reduced to its recoverable amount. In allocating the
                    額。於分配減值虧損時，減值                                 impairment loss, the impairment loss is allocated
                    虧損首先分配至削減任何商譽                                 first to reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill
                   （如適用）的賬面值，然後根據                                 (if applicable) and then to the other assets on a
                    各資產於現金產生單位或一組                                 pro-rata basis based on the carrying amount of
                    現金產生單位的賬面值按比例                                 each asset in the unit or the group of CGUs. An
                    分配至其他資產。減值虧損即                                 impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit
                    時於損益中確認。                                      or loss.

           遞延稅項乃按合併財務報表內                Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences
           資產與負債賬面值與計算應納                between the carrying amounts of assets and
           稅利潤所使用相應稅基的暫時                liabilities in the consolidated financial statements
           差額確認。遞延稅項負債一般                and the corresponding tax bases used in the
           就所有應課稅暫時差額確認。                computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities
           若有可能取得可利用可扣減暫                are generally recognised for all taxable temporary
           時差額抵銷應納稅利潤時，一                differences. Deferred tax assets are generally
           般就所有可扣減暫時差額確認                recognised for all deductible temporary differences
           遞延稅項資產。倘暫時差額自                to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits
           不影響應納稅利潤或會計利潤                will be available against which those deductible
           的交易項下其他資產及負債                 temporary differences can be utilised. Such
           的初始確認所產生（業務合併                deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised
           所產生者除外），且於交易時                if the temporary difference arises from the initial
           並無產生相等的應課稅及可扣                recognition (other than in a business combination)
           減暫時差額，則該等遞延稅項                of other assets and liabilities in a transaction that
           資產及負債不予確認。此外，                affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting
           倘暫時差額產生自商譽的初始                profit and at the time of the transaction does not
           確認，則不會確認遞延稅項負                give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary
           債。                           differences. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are
                                        not recognised if the temporary difference arises
                                        from the initial recognition of goodwill.


           遞延稅項負債乃按與子公司之                Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable
           投資有關的應課稅暫時差額確                temporary differences associated with investments
           認，除非本集團可控制暫時                 in subsidiaries, except where the Group is able to
           差額的撥回及暫時差額很可能                control the reversal of the temporary difference
           於可見將來不會撥回。與該等                and it is probable that the temporary difference
           投資及權益相關的可扣減暫時                will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred
           差額所產生的遞延稅項資產，                tax assets arising from deductible temporary
           僅於很可能有足夠應納稅利潤                differences associated with such investments and
           抵銷暫時差額而利用其利益且                interests are only recognised to the extent that
           預計於可見將來可以撥回時確                it is probable that there will be sufficient taxable

                    債後於本集團資產之剩餘權益                                 a residual interest in the assets of the Group after
                    的任何合同。本公司發行的權                                 deducting all of its liabilities. Equity instruments

     於應用附註3 所述的本集團會計政策            In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which
     時，本公司董事須對未能依循其他途             are described in note 3, the directors of the Company are
     徑取得的資產及負債賬面值作出判              required to make judgments, estimates and assumptions
     斷、估計及假設。估計及相關假設乃             about the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that
     根據過往經驗及其他視為相關的因素             are not readily apparent from other sources. The estimates
     作出。實際結果可能有別於該等估計。            and underlying assumptions are based on historical
                                  experience and other factors that are considered to be
                                  relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.


     本集團持續檢討估計及相關假設。倘             The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed
     修訂會計估計僅影響某一期間，則於             on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are
     修訂有關估計的期間內確認修訂；倘             recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised
     修訂影響本期間及未來期間，則於作             if the revision affects only that period, or in the period of
     出修訂的期間及未來期間確認有關修             the revision and future periods if the revision affects both
     訂。                           current and future periods.

     信貸虧損要求應用重大判斷，包括預             measurement of ECL requires the application of significant
     期未來現金流量及前瞻性宏觀因素。             judgment which include the expected future cash flows
     鑒於相關金融工具之預期未來信貸虧             and forward-looking macroeconomic factors. The loss
     損，虧損撥備金額計量為資產之賬面             allowance amount is measured as the difference between
     值與估計未來現金流量之現值的差              the asset’s carrying amount and the present value of
     額。相關金融工具之信貸風險評估涉             estimated future cash flows with the consideration of
     及較高程度之估計及不明朗因素。當             expected future credit loss of the respective financial
     實際未來現金流量低於或高於所預期             instrument. The assessment of the credit risk of the
     者，因此可能出現重大減值虧損或重             respective financial instrument involves high degree of
     大減值虧損撥回。                     estimation and uncertainty. When the actual future cash
                                  flows are less than expected or more than expected,

      根據銷售協議，客戶有權根據向客戶             Pursuant to the sales agreements, the customers are
      提供的實際銷量向本集團收取銷量折             entitled to receive sales volume rebates from the Group
      扣。於向客戶交付貨品後，本集團將             based on the actual sales volume to the customers.
      按月根據實際銷售量釐定應付予客戶             After the delivery of goods to the customers, the Group
      的銷量折扣，並將應付金額計入其他             would determine the sales volume rebates payable to the
      應付款項（附註21）。其後，本集團將           customers based on the actual sales volume on a monthly
      定期與客戶確認銷量折扣的結算方式             basis and the payable amount is included in the other
      （現金或存貨）。倘客戶與本集團確認            payables (note 21). Subsequently, the Group would confirm
      以存貨結算銷量折扣，應付客戶款項             with the customers the settlement method of the sales
      將重新分類至合同負債。                  volume rebates (either by cash or inventory) on a regular
                                   basis. If the customers confirm with the Group to settle
                                   the sales volume rebates by inventory, the payables to
                                   customers would be reclassified to contract liabilities.


      本集團並無原預期年期超過一年的收             The Group has no revenue contract that has an original
      益合同，因此管理層根據香港財務報             expected duration more than one year, thus management
      告準則第15號應用實際權宜方法，並            applied practical expedient under HKFRS 15 and are
      無披露分配至截至報告期末尚未達成             not disclosing the aggregate amount of the transaction
      或部分達成的履約責任的交易價格總             price allocated to the performance obligation that are
      額。                           unsatisfied or partially satisfied as of the end of the

27.   退休福利計劃                     27.     RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES
      本集團根據強制性公積金計劃（「強制                  The Group operates a defined contribution retirement
      性公積金計劃」）為合資格參與強制性                  benefits scheme under the Mandatory Provident Fund
      公積金計劃的僱員設立一項界定供款                   Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) for employees who are eligible
      退休福利計劃。強制性公積金計劃的                   to participate in the MPF Scheme. The assets of the MPF
      資產與本集團的資產分開，並由受託                   Scheme are held separately from those of the Group, in
      人控制的基金持有。本集團按相關工                   fund under the control of trustees. The Group contributes
      資成本的若干百分比向強制性公積金                   certain percent of relevant payroll costs to the MPF
      計劃作出供款，而該等供款由僱員作                   Scheme, which contribution is matched by employees.
      出。


      本公司於中國成立的子公司的僱員為                   The employees of the Company’s subsidiaries established
      中國政府運作的國家管理退休福利計                   in the PRC are members of a state-managed retirement
      劃的成員。子公司須按工資成本的若                   benefit scheme operated by the PRC government. The
      干百分比向退休福利計劃供款，以為                   subsidiaries are required to contribute certain percentage
      福利提供資金。本集團就退休福利計                   of payroll costs to the retirement benefits schemes to fund
      劃的唯一責任為作出特定供款。                     the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect
                                         to the retirement benefit schemes is to make the specific
                                         contributions.

      無遭沒收供款（2023 年：零）可用於                there was no forfeited contribution available to reduce
      降低供款水平。                            level of contributions (2023: Nil).

            本集團的內部信貸風險評級包                                         The Group’s internal credit risk grading assessment
            括以下類別：                                                comprises the following categories:

            內部信貸評級                    描述
            Internal credit rating    Description

            低風險                       對手方違約風險低，且並無任何逾期金額。
            Low risk                  The counterparty has a low risk of default and does not have any past-due amounts.
            監察名單                      債務人恆常於到期日後償還，但通常全數結算。
            Watch list                Debtor frequently repays after due dates but usually settle in full.
            呆賬                        自通過內部或外部資源獲得的信息初始確認以來，信貸風險已顯著增加。
            Doubtful                  There have been significant increases in credit risk since initial recognition through
                                      information developed internally or external resources.
            虧損                        有證據顯示該資產已發生信貸減值。
            Loss                      There is evidence indicating the asset is credit-impaired.
            撇銷                        有證據顯示債務人陷入嚴重財務困難且本集團預期無實際收回款項的可能。
            Write-off                 There is evidence indicating the debtor is in severe financial difficulty and the Group
                                      has no realistic prospect of recovery.
